## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] Hi, I'm Ducter, your partner for work on your computers. For example, I can get a failing test in one of your repos fixed and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 어떤 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 고쳐 달라고 하시면 됩니다.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 안녕히 주무세요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を保証する」通信、UDPは「速さを優先して届くことは保証しない」通信です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para poner tu rama local o privada al día con la rama base y mantener el historial lineal antes de integrarla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque reescribir commits publicados rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、保证可靠送达和顺序的；UDP 是无连接、只管尽快发出去、不保证送达和顺序。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is still yours alone and you want a clean, linear history before sharing it, such as tidying up your commits or catching up with main. Merge when others have already pulled the branch or you need to keep its true history, since rebasing rewrites commits they may depend on.
```

### group-split-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: ~/dev/ducter 的登录页要重做：先把登录表单换成新的输入框组件，然后接上短信验证码，最后补登录页的端到端测试。一件做完再做下一件。

Run the test suite (or the relevant subset if the full suite is very slow) and report the actual command and its result. ⏎  ⏎ Done criteria: the default is 30s; no code, doc, or test still states the old default; the tests pass, with the command and its output reported as evidence; a short summary of every file changed. ⏎  ⏎ Out of scope: do not push, open a PR, or merge. ### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays the log on restart to recover committed changes and keep the data consistent.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我去把 compaction PR 里 review 提到的锁粒度改细，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要求：
- 把 review 里关于锁粒度的意见落实：缩小锁的持有范围，只在真正需要互斥的地方加锁，减少 compaction 期间对读写的阻塞。
- 保持现有行为不变，已有测试要继续通过；如果需要，补一个验证并发读写不被整段阻塞的测试。
- 在原来的分支上提交，不合并、不推送、不改 PR 的状态（仍是 draft）。
- 完成后报告：改了哪些加锁位置、测试结果、还有没有 review 里提到但没做的点。

范围外：不改 compaction 的其他逻辑，不合并到主分支，不推送，不发评论。
```

  function render(nights) {

    empty.hidden = true;
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    latest.textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';

      var stalls = (n.stalls === undefined || n.stalls === null) ? '' : ' · compaction stalls ' + n.stalls;
      return '<div class="k-row">' +
        '<div><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(n.night) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + esc(n.p99) + stalls + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + esc(n.writes) + ' writes/s</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }
  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('nights')).then(render);
  }
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms"},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

```

  [partner message] 做好了，记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3437 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">下面按天记每一笔，合计自动算</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">按天</h2>
<div id="days"></div>
<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>哪天</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="如 午饭、打车"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>多少钱</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const KEY = 'expenses';
let items = [];
let openI = null;

function esc(s) {
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c]));
}
function money(n) { return '¥' + Number(n).toFixed(2); }

function render() {
  const total = items.reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b.amount), 0);
  const tot = document.getElementById('total');
  const sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  if (!items.length) {
    tot.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没记</sp … getElementById('days').innerHTML = html ||
    '<div class="k-empty">还没有一笔。展开下面的「记一笔」记第一笔。</div>';

  ducter.summary(items.length ? items.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + money(total) : '还没记');
}

function save() { ducter.set(KEY, items); render(); }

document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const day = document.getElementById('day').value.trim();
  const what = document.getElementById('what').value.trim();
  const amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value);
  if (!day || !what || isNaN(amount)) return;
  items.push({ day, what, amount });
  document.getElementById('what').value = '';
  document.getElementById('amt').value = '';
  save();
});

document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    items.splice(Number(del.getAttribute('data-del')), 1);
    openI = null;
    save();
    return;
  }
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row[data-i]');
  if (row) {
    const i = Number(row.getAttribute('data-i'));
    openI = openI === i ? null : i;
    render();
  }
});

ducter.onChange(() => { items = ducter.get(KEY) || []; render(); });
items = ducter.get(KEY) || [];
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

    resetForm();
    render();
  }

  saveBtn.onclick = save;
  cancelBtn.onclick = resetForm;
  delBtn.onclick = function () {
    if (editIdx >= 0) {
      feeds.splice(editIdx, 1);
      ducter.set('feeds', feeds);
    }
    resetForm();
    render();
  };

  function load(v) {
    feeds = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }

  var v = ducter.get('feeds');
  if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') v.then(load); else load(v);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    var nv = ducter.get('feeds');
    if (nv && typeof nv.then === 'function') nv.then(load); else load(nv);
  });

  resetForm();
})();

  function render() {

    w.innerHTML = waiting.map(row).join('');
    o.innerHTML = items.filter(i => i.group === 'other').map(row).join('');
    const open = waiting.filter(i => !landed.includes(i.id)).length;
    document.getElementById('count').textContent = open;
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = open === 0
      ? 'all landed' : 'tick each one off as it lands';
  }

  function toggle(id) {
    landed = landed.includes(id) ? landed.filter(x => x !== id) : landed.concat(id);
    Promise.resolve(ducter.set('landed', landed));
    render();
  }

  document.addEventListener('change', e => {
    if (e.target.type === 'checkbox') toggle(e.target.closest('.k-row').dataset.id);
  });

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => {
    landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  });
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(() => Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(v => {
    landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }));
  ducter.summary('3 waiting');
  render();
